This is a known Java 9+ compatibility issue.\n\n## Error Message\n\n```\nCould not find artifact com.sun:tools:jar:11.0.27 at specified path /usr/lib/jvm/java-11-openjdk-amd64/../lib/tools.jar\n```\n\n## Root Cause\n\n1. **Java Version**: The build is running on Java 11 (OpenJDK 11.0.27)\n\n2. **tools.jar Removal**: In Java 9+, the `tools.jar` file was removed. It no longer exists as\n   a separate JAR file. The classes that were in tools.jar are now part of the JDK's module\n   system (specifically the `jdk.compiler` module).\n\n3. **Outdated Dependency**: The `compile-testing` library (versions 0.6, 0.7, 0.8 used in\n   this project) has a transitive dependency on `com.sun:tools:jar` which expects the\n   `tools.jar` file to exist at `${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar`.\n\n4. **Outdated Parent POM Profile**: The parent pom.xml has profiles that check for the\n   existence of `tools.jar` but doesn't have a profile for Java 9+ where tools.jar\n   doesn't exist.\n\n## Affected Modules\n\n1. **common/pom.xml**: Uses compile-testing version 0.7\n2. **factory/pom.xml**: Uses compile-testing version 0.8\n3. **service/pom.xml**: Uses compile-testing version 0.6\n4. **value/pom.xml**: Uses compile-testing version 0.6\n\n## Solution\n\n### Fix 1: Update compile-testing dependency\n\nUpdate the `compile-testing` library to version 0.19 in all module pom.xml files.\nVersion 0.15+ of compile-testing removed the dependency on `com.sun:tools` and\nsupports Java 9+.\n\nAffected files:\n- common/pom.xml: Change version 0.7 -> 0.19\n- factory/pom.xml: Change version 0.8 -> 0.19\n- service/pom.xml: Change version 0.6 -> 0.19\n- value/pom.xml: Change version 0.6 -> 0.19\n\n### Fix 2: Add Java 9+ profile to parent pom.xml\n\nAdd a new profile in the parent pom.xml that activates for Java 9+ environments.\nThis profile should skip the tools.jar configuration since it's not needed.\n\n### Fix 3: Update Java version property\n\nUpdate the java.version property from 1.6 to 1.8 (minimum) since Java 11 cannot\ncompile to Java 1.6 bytecode using source 1.6 syntax. Java 11 supports targeting\nback to Java 6 bytecode but the source/target 1.6 is deprecated.\n\n## Patch Files to Create\n\n1. patch_1.diff - Update common/pom.xml (compile-testing 0.7 -> 0.19)\n2. patch_2.diff - Update factory/pom.xml (compile-testing 0.8 -> 0.19)\n3. patch_3.diff - Update service/pom.xml (compile-testing 0.6 -> 0.19)\n4. patch_4.diff - Update value/pom.xml (compile-testing 0.6 -> 0.19)\n5. patch_5.diff - Update parent pom.xml (add Java 9+ profile and update java.version)\n"}}
